_post_or_404(conn, post_id)\n    posts.set_status(conn, post_id, \"draft\")\n    flash(request, f\"Post “{post['title']}” unpublished.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/content\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/delete\", dependencies=csrf_required)\ndef delete_post(post_id: int, request: Request, conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    post = _post_or_404(conn, post_id)\n    posts.delete(conn, post_id)\n    flash(request, f\"Post “{post['title']}” deleted.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/content\", status_code=303)\n\"\"\"Shared web plumbing: templates, sessions, the login check and CSRF.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for an anonymous visitor; the app turns it into a redirect.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.database_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request, conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    \"\"\"The signed-in, active user, or LoginRequired. Guards every admin route.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = users.get_by_id(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\nclass Refused(Exception):\n    \"\"\"The signed-in user may not do this; the app turns it into the refusal page.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, message: str = \"Only the Head Coach can do this.\"):\n        super().__init__(message)\n        self.message = message\n\n\ndef require_admin(user=Depends(current_user)):\n    \"\"\"Dependency for admin-only routes: the signed-in user must be an admin.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise Refused()\n    return user\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing form: reject a missing/bad token.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = form.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not isinstance(sent, str) or not expected\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Missing or invalid CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef flash(request: Request, text: str, kind: str = \"ok\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Queue a message to show on the next page (success \"ok\" or \"error\").\"\"\"\n    request.session[\"flash\"] = {\"kind\": kind, \"text\": text}\n\n\ndef render(request: Request, name: str, context: dict | None = None,\n           status_code: int = 200, user=None):\n    ctx = {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n           \"user\": user, \"flash\": request.session.pop(\"flash\", None)}\n    ctx.update(context or {})\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, name, ctx, status_code=status_code)\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import db, pages, posts, settings\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\n\nHOME_SLUG = \"home\"\nEVENTS_SLUG = \"alumni-events\"\nSECTION_SLUGS = (\"team-schedule\", EVENTS_SLUG, \"alumni-network\")\nHOME_COUNT = 3  # Team Updates and Events shown on Home\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nnav ul { list-style: none; margin: .5rem 0 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: .25rem 1rem; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef _filename(slug: str) -> str:\n    if slug == HOME_SLUG:\n        return \"index.html\"\n    # \"index\" would overwrite the Home page.\n    return \"index-page.html\" if slug == \"index\" else f\"{slug}.html\"\n\n\ndef _navigation(published: list[sqlite3.Row]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Home first, then the other published pages alphabetically by title.\"\"\"\n    ordered = sorted(published, key=lambda p: (p[\"slug\"] != HOME_SLUG, p[\"title\"].lower()))\n    return [{\"title\": p[\"title\"], \"href\": _filename(p[\"slug\"])} for p in ordered]\n\n\ndef _post_filenames(published: list[sqlite3.Row], taken: set[str]) -> dict[int, str]:\n    \"\"\"One file per post. A page's file always wins a clash; the post is renamed.\"\"\"\n    names: dict[int, str] = {}\n    for post in published:\n        name = f\"post-{post['slug']}.html\"\n        while name in taken:\n            name = name.removesuffix(\".html\") + \"-post.html\"\n        taken.add(name)\n        names[post[\"id\"]] = name\n    return names\n\n\ndef _summary(post: sqlite3.Row, names: dict[int, str]) -> dict[str, str]:\n    return {\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"href\": names[post[\"id\"]], \"date\": post[\"created_at\"][:10]}\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None,\n                database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    database_path = database_path or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    db.init_db(database_path)\n    conn = db.connect(database_path)\n    try:\n        published = pages.list_published(conn)\n        published_posts = posts.list_published(conn)  # newest first\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    nav = _navigation(published)\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    page_files = {_filename(p[\"slug\"]) for p in published} | {\"index.html\", \"style.css\"}\n    post_files = _post_filenames(published_posts, set(page_files))\n    link_targets = page_files | set(post_files.values())\n    by_slug = {p[\"slug\"]: p for p in published}\n\n    events = [_summary(p, post_files) for p in published_posts if p[\"post_type\"] == \"event\"]\n    updates = [_summary(p, post_files) for p in published_posts\n               if p[\"post_type\"] == \"team_update\"]\n    home_sections = {\n        \"updates\": updates[:HOME_COUNT], \"events\": events[:HOME_COUNT],\n        \"section_links\": [{\"title\": by_slug[s][\"title\"], \"href\": _filename(s)}\n                          for s in SECTION_SLUGS if s in by_slug]}\n    sections_for = {HOME_SLUG: home_sections, EVENTS_SLUG: {\"events\": events}}\n\n    common = dict(site_title=settings.SITE_TITLE, nav=nav, css_path=\"style.css\",\n                  home_path=\"index.html\")\n    for page in published:\n        (out / _filename(page[\"slug\"])).write_text(\n            env.get_template(\"public/page.html\").render(\n                title=page[\"title\"],\n                body=render_markdown(page[\"body\"], link_targets=link_targets),\n                **sections_for.get(page[\"slug\"], {}), **common))\n\n    if HOME_SLUG not in by_slug:\n        (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n            env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n                title=settings.SITE_TITLE, **home_sections, **common))\n\n    for post in published_posts:\n        (out / post_files[post[\"id\"]]).write_text(\n            env.get_template(\"public/post.html\").render(\n                title=post[\"title\"], type_label=posts.POST_TYPES[post[\"post_type\"]],\n                date=post[\"created_at\"][:10],\n                body=render_markdown(post[\"body\"], link_targets=link_targets), **common))\n    return out\n{% if section_links %}\n<ul>{% for link in section_links %}<li><a href=\"{{ link.href }}\">{{ link.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n{% endif %}\n{% if updates is defined %}\n<section>\n  <h2>Team Updates</h2>\n  {% if updates %}\n  <ul>{% for p in updates %}<li><a href=\"{{ p.href }}\">{{ p.title }}</a> <small>{{ p.date }}</small></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}<p>No team updates yet.</p>{% endif %}\n</section>\n{% endif %}\n{% if events is defined %}\n<section>\n  <h2>Events</h2>\n  {% if events %}\n  <ul>{% for p in events %}<li><a href=\"{{ p.href }}\">{{ p.title }}</a> <small>{{ p.date }}</small></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}<p>No events yet.</p>{% endif %}\n</section>\n{% endif %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {{ body | safe }}\n  {% include \"public/_sections.html\" %}\n{% endblock %}\n# ADR-003: Alumni Listings are a separate type; emails are stored but never published\n\n**Status:** accepted\n**Date:** 2026-10-04\n\n## Context\n\nThe client wants an opt-in alumni contact list. Alumni cannot log in, so the Second\nEditor records each alumnus's consent. The public site is static on GitHub Pages:\nanything published is readable by anyone, including scrapers. The manual's content\ntypes are Posts and Pages, and neither has a field for consent. Options considered:\na public list with emails, obfuscated emails, names only with a contact path, and a\nMarkdown page typed by hand.\n\n## Decision\n\nAn alumnus's entry is a **Listing**, a third content type with name, email, optional\nclass year, consent date, and who recorded it. Only Listings with consent are\nexported, and only their **names** reach `site/`. Emails stay in the database. The\npublic Alumni Network page carries one Alumni Contact Line telling visitors to email\nthe Second Editor, who introduces them to the alumnus.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- The exporter never reads the email field, so a test can assert that no alumnus\n  email appears anywhere in `site/`.\n- Removing a Listing or withdrawing consent removes the name at the next publish,\n  with no leftover text in a page body.\n- An extra model, admin screen, and exporter step are needed beyond Posts and Pages.\n- Visitors cannot contact an alumnus directly. The Second Editor is a manual go-between.\n- Once names are published to GitHub Pages they may be cached or indexed, so\n  withdrawal cannot recall a name already published.\n\n## Why this is an ADR\n\nIt is hard to reverse (the model and the exporter both depend on it), surprising\nwithout context (the manual lists only Posts and Pages), and the result of a real\ntrade-off between findability and the privacy the client asked for.\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import db, users\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\ndef _csrf_from(response) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', response.text)\n    assert match, \"page has no CSRF token\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef database_path(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"A fresh SQLite file with the two demo users, so tests never share state.\"\"\"\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    db.init_db(path)\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    for role, name in ((\"admin\", \"Matt Burdette\"), (\"editor\", \"Joe\")):\n        users.create_user(conn, email=DEMO_USERS[role][\"email\"], name=name,\n                          password=DEMO_USERS[role][\"password\"], role=role)\n    conn.close()\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(database_path) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(database_path):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n        token = _csrf_from(c.get(\"/login\"))\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\"\"\"T04: Events and Team Updates. Each test names the criterion it covers.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import db, pages\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import _csrf_from\n\n\ndef _token(c) -> str:\n    return _csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n\n\ndef _create(c, title=\"Reunion\", body=\"Body\", status=\"draft\", post_type=\"event\",\n            slug=\"\", csrf=True):\n    data = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body, \"status\": status}\n    if post_type is not None:\n        data[\"post_type\"] = post_type\n    if csrf:\n        data[\"csrf_token\"] = _token(c)\n    return c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef _post_id(response) -> int:\n    match = re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)/edit\", response.headers[\"location\"])\n    assert match, response.headers\n    return int(match.group(1))\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"role\", [\"admin\", \"editor\"])\ndef test_editor_can_create_post_with_generated_slug(client_as, role):\n    \"\"\"Create a post with a generated slug, author and timestamps; success message.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(role)\n    response = _create(c, title=\"Spring Reunion & BBQ!\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    edit = c.get(response.headers[\"location\"])\n    assert 'value=\"spring-reunion-bbq\"' in edit.text\n    assert \"saved\" in edit.text.lower()\n    assert \"Created\" in edit.text and \"Updated\" in edit.text and \"Author\" in edit.text\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_editable_and_unique(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = _create(c, title=\"A\", slug=\"Custom Slug\")\n    assert 'value=\"custom-slug\"' in c.get(response.headers[\"location\"]).text\n    again = _create(c, title=\"B\", slug=\"custom-slug\")\n    assert again.status_code == 409\n    assert \"already used\" in again.text\n\n\ndef test_post_type_is_required_and_must_be_valid(client_as):\n    \"\"\"Post Type is required, and is Event or Team Update.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert _create(c, post_type=None).status_code == 422\n    assert _create(c, post_type=\"\").status_code == 422\n    assert _create(c, post_type=\"news\").status_code == 422\n    assert _create(c, post_type=\"team_update\").status_code == 303","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"b26184e6-ad33-462d-8d43-e095e7036df0","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":7},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c8826958-1273-416c-9c49-b621bdae6adf","session_id":"9d3ee875-b856-4c5f-8263-d5e667d38523","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"c452e23c-792e-4dd7-91fc-5a3784f2bd13","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

1rem; padding: .75rem 1rem; background: #4b2e83; color: #fff; }\n    header a, header button { color: #fff; }\n    header form { display: inline; margin: 0; }\n    header button { background: none; border: 1px solid #fff; border-radius: 4px;\n                    padding: .25rem .6rem; cursor: pointer; }\n    main { max-width: 52rem; margin: 1.5rem auto; padding: 0 1rem; }\n    label { display: width: 100%; padding: .5rem; box-sizing: border-box; }\n    .msg-error { background: #fde8e8; border: 1px solid #c53030; padding: .6rem; }\n    .msg-ok { background: #e6f4ea; border: 1px solid #2f855a; padding: .6rem; }\n  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header>\n    <a href=\"/admin\">Kenyon Baseball Alumni · Admin</a>\n    {% if user %}<nav><a href=\"/admin/content\">Content</a> · <a href=\"/admin/pages\">Pages</a> · <a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New post</a>{% if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\" %} · <a href=\"/admin/users\">Users</a>{% endif %}</nav>{% endif %}\n    {% if user %}\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n      <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n      <span>Signed in as {{ user[\"name\"] }} ({{ user[\"role\"] }})</span>\n      <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n    </form>\n    {% endif %}\n  </header>\n  <main>\n  {% if flash %}<p class=\"msg-{{ flash.kind }}\" role=\"{{ 'alert' if flash.kind == 'error' else 'status' }}\">{{ flash.text }}</p>{% endif %}\n  {% block content %}{% endblock %}\n  </main>\n</body>\n</html>\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ 'Edit post' if post else 'New post' }}</h1>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ '/admin/posts/%d' % post.id if post else '/admin/posts' }}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    {% if post %}<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"post_id\" value=\"{{ post.id }}\">{% endif %}\n    <label for=\"post_type\">Post Type</label>\n    <select id=\"post_type\" name=\"post_type\" required>\n      {% for value, label in post_types.items() %}\n      <option value=\"{{ value }}\" {{ 'selected' if form.post_type == value }}>{{ label }}</option>\n      {% endfor %}\n    </select>\n    <label for=\"title\">Title</label>\n    <input id=\"title\" name=\"title\" value=\"{{ form.title }}\" required>\n    <label for=\"slug\">Slug (the web address; leave blank to generate it from the title)</label>\n    <input id=\"slug\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ form.slug }}\">\n    <label for=\"body\">Body (Markdown)</label>\n    <textarea id=\"body\" name=\"body\" rows=\"14\">{{ form.body }}</textarea>\n    <label for=\"status\">Status</label>\n    <select id=\"status\" name=\"status\">\n      {% for s in ('draft', 'published') %}\n      <option value=\"{{ s }}\" {{ 'selected' if form.status == s }}>{{ s }}</option>\n      {% endfor %}\n    </select>\n    <p>\n      <button type=\"submit\">Save</button>\n      <button type=\"submit\" formaction=\"/admin/posts/preview\">Preview</button>\n      <a href=\"/admin/content\">Back to content</a>\n    </p>\n  </form>\n  {% if post %}\n  <dl>\n    <dt>Author</dt><dd>{{ post.author_name }}</dd>\n    <dt>Created</dt><dd>{{ post.created_at }}</dd>\n    <dt>Updated</dt><dd>{{ post.updated_at }}</dd>\n  </dl>\n  {% endif %}\n  {% if preview is not none %}\n  <h2>Preview</h2>\n  <div id=\"preview\">{{ preview | safe }}</div>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Content</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/pages/new\">New page</a> · <a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New post</a></p>\n  <form method=\"get\" action=\"/admin/content\">\n    <label for=\"status\">Status</label>\n    <select id=\"status\" name=\"status\">\n      <option value=\"\">All</option>\n      {% for s in statuses %}<option value=\"{{ s }}\" {{ 'selected' if status == s }}>{{ s }}</option>{% endfor %}\n    </select>\n    <label for=\"type\">Type</label>\n    <select id=\"type\" name=\"type\">\n      <option value=\"\">All</option>\n      {% for value, label in types.items() %}<option value=\"{{ value }}\" {{ 'selected' if type == value }}>{{ label }}</option>{% endfor %}\n    </select>\n    <p><button type=\"submit\">Filter</button></p>\n  </form>\n  {% if items %}\n  <table>\n    <thead><tr><th>Title</th><th>Type</th><th>Status</th><th>Last update</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n    {% for i in items %}\n      {% set base = '/admin/pages' if i.kind == 'page' else '/admin/posts' %}\n      <tr>\n        <td><a href=\"{{ base }}/{{ i.id }}/edit\">{{ i.title }}</a></td>\n        <td>{{ i.type_label }}</td>\n        <td>{{ i.status }}</td>\n        <td>{{ i.updated_at }}</td>\n        <td>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ base }}/{{ i.id }}/{{ 'unpublish' if i.status == 'published' else 'publish' }}\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">{{ 'Mark draft' if i.status == 'published' else 'Mark published' }}</button>\n          </form>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ base }}/{{ i.id }}/delete\" onsubmit=\"return confirm('Delete this item?')\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">Delete</button>\n          </form>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n    {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>Nothing matches.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import db, pages, posts, settings, users\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    db.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    conn = db.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    try:\n        for email, name, password, role in (\n            (\"admin@example.test\", \"Matt Burdette\", admin_pw, \"admin\"),\n            (\"editor@example.test\", \"Joe\", editor_pw, \"editor\"),\n        ):\n            if users.get_by_email(conn, email) is None:\n                users.create_user(conn, email=email, name=name,\n                                  password=password, role=role)\n                print(f\"Created {role}: {email}\")\n            else:\n                print(f\"Exists: {email}\")\n        admin = users.get_by_email(conn, \"admin@example.test\")\n        if admin is not None and not pages.list_pages(conn):\n            for title, body, locked in (\n                (\"Home\", \"Welcome, Kenyon baseball alumni and fans.\", False),\n                (\"Team Schedule\", \"| Date | Opponent |\\n|---|---|\\n| TBD | TBD |\", True),\n                (\"Alumni Events\", \"Gatherings for alumni of the program.\", False),\n                (\"Alumni Network\", \"Alumni who chose to be listed.\", False),\n                (\"About\", \"About the Kenyon Varsity Baseball alumni site.\", False),\n            ):\n                page_id = pages.create(conn, title=title, slug=pages.slugify(title),\n                                       body=body, status=\"published\",\n                                       author_id=admin[\"id\"])\n                if locked:\n                    pages.set_locked(conn, page_id, True)\n            print(\"Created pages (Team Schedule is Locked)\")\n        # A demo database made before T05 has an unlocked Team Schedule.\n        schedule = conn.execute(\"SELECT id FROM pages WHERE slug = 'team-schedule'\").fetchone()\n        if schedule is not None:\n            pages.set_locked(conn, schedule[\"id\"], True)\n        editor = users.get_by_email(conn, \"editor@example.test\")\n        if editor is not None and not posts.list_posts(conn):\n            for title, post_type, status, body in (\n                (\"Spring Reunion Weekend\", \"event\", \"published\",\n                 \"Join us on campus for the annual alumni reunion.\"),\n                (\"Game-Watch at the Pub\", \"event\", \"published\",\n                 \"Watch the conference final together.\"),\n                (\"Fall Ball Wrap-Up\", \"team_update\", \"published\",\n                 \"The team finished fall ball with a strong week.\"),\n                (\"Draft: Banquet Details\", \"event\", \"draft\", \"Still to be confirmed.\"),\n            ):\n                posts.create(conn, title=title, slug=posts.slugify(title), body=body,\n                             status=status, post_type=post_type, author_id=editor[\"id\"])\n            print(\"Created sample posts\")\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n    # TODO (later tickets): listings.\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n# Copy to .env (which is gitignored) and change the values.\nCMS_SECRET_KEY=change-me-to-a-long-random-string\nCMS_SITE_TITLE=Knox County Historical Society\nCMS_DATABASE=cms.db\n# Demo accounts created by scripts/seed_demo.py\nCMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me-admin\nCMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD=change-me-editor\n# Set after you deploy, e.g. https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nCMS_BASE_PATH=\n\"\"\"Admin: one list of pages and posts together, filtered by status and type.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request\n\nfrom app import pages, posts\nfrom app.web import current_user, get_db, render\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/content\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n\n# The \"type\" filter: Page, or one of the Post Types.\nTYPES = {\"page\": \"Page\", **posts.POST_TYPES}\n\n\ndef _items(conn: sqlite3.Connection, status: str, type_: str) -> list[dict]:\n    items = [{\"kind\": \"page\", \"type\": \"page\", \"type_label\": \"Page\", **dict(p)}\n             for p in pages.list_pages(conn)]\n    items += [{\"kind\": \"post\", \"type\": p[\"post_type\"],\n               \"type_label\": posts.POST_TYPES[p[\"post_type\"]], **dict(p)}\n              for p in posts.list_posts(conn)]\n    items = [i for i in items\n             if (not status or i[\"status\"] == status) and (not type_ or i[\"type\"] == type_)]\n    return sorted(items, key=lambda i: (i[\"updated_at\"], i[\"id\"]), reverse=True)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef content_list(request: Request, status: str = \"\", type: str = \"\",\n                 user=Depends(current_user), conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    status = status if status in pages.STATUSES else \"\"\n    type_ = type if type in TYPES else \"\"\n    return render(request, \"admin/content_list.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Content\", \"items\": _items(conn, status, type_),\n                   \"status\": status, \"type\": type_, \"statuses\": pages.STATUSES,\n                   \"types\": TYPES}, user=user)\n101:    for route in _users_routes(create_app(database_path)):\n126:def _users_routes(app):\n127:    \"\"\"Every Users APIRoute, including those in included routers (FastAPI nests them).\"\"\"\n129:    for route in app.routes:\n130:        nested = getattr(route, \"original_router\", None)\n131:        for r in ([route] if isinstance(route, APIRoute) else nested.routes if nested else []):\n140:    routes = _users_routes(app)\n141:    assert len(routes) >= 5\n144:    for route in routes:\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n base }}/{{ i.id }}/edit\">{{ i.title }}</a></td>\n        <td>{{ i.type_label }}</td>\n        <td>{{ i.status }}</td>\n        <td>{{ i.updated_at }}</td>\n        <td>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ base }}/{{ i.id }}/{{ 'unpublish' if i.status == 'published' else 'publish' }}\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">{{ 'Mark draft' if i.status == 'published' else 'Mark published' }}</button>\n          </form>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ base }}/{{ i.id }}/delete\" onsubmit=\"return confirm('Delete this item?')\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">Delete</button>\n          </form>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n    {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>Nothing matches.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import db, pages, posts, settings, users\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    db.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    conn = db.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    try:\n        for email, name, password, role in (\n            (\"admin@example.test\", \"Matt Burdette\", admin_pw, \"admin\"),\n            (\"editor@example.test\", \"Joe\", editor_pw, \"editor\"),\n        ):\n            if users.get_by_email(conn, email) is None:\n                users.create_user(conn, email=email, name=name,\n                                  password=password, role=role)\n                print(f\"Created {role}: {email}\")\n            else:\n                print(f\"Exists: {email}\")\n        admin = users.get_by_email(conn, \"admin@example.test\")\n        if admin is not None and not pages.list_pages(conn):\n            for title, body, locked in (\n                (\"Home\", \"Welcome, Kenyon baseball alumni and fans.\", False),\n                (\"Team Schedule\", \"| Date | Opponent |\\n|---|---|\\n| TBD | TBD |\", True),\n                (\"Alumni Events\", \"Gatherings for alumni of the program.\", False),\n                (\"Alumni Network\", \"Alumni who chose to be listed.\", False),\n                (\"About\", \"About the Kenyon Varsity Baseball alumni site.\", False),\n            ):\n                page_id = pages.create(conn, title=title, slug=pages.slugify(title),\n                                       body=body, status=\"published\",\n                                       author_id=admin[\"id\"])\n                if locked:\n                    pages.set_locked(conn, page_id, True)\n            print(\"Created pages (Team Schedule is Locked)\")\n        # A demo database made before T05 has an unlocked Team Schedule.\n        schedule = conn.execute(\"SELECT id FROM pages WHERE slug = 'team-schedule'\").fetchone()\n        if schedule is not None:\n            pages.set_locked(conn, schedule[\"id\"], True)\n        editor = users.get_by_email(conn, \"editor@example.test\")\n        if editor is not None and not posts.list_posts(conn):\n            for title, post_type, status, body in (\n                (\"Spring Reunion Weekend\", \"event\", \"published\",\n                 \"Join us on campus for the annual alumni reunion.\"),\n                (\"Game-Watch at the Pub\", \"event\", \"published\",\n                 \"Watch the conference final together.\"),\n                (\"Fall Ball Wrap-Up\", \"team_update\", \"published\",\n                 \"The team finished fall ball with a strong week.\"),\n                (\"Draft: Banquet Details\", \"event\", \"draft\", \"Still to be confirmed.\"),\n            ):\n                posts.create(conn, title=title, slug=posts.slugify(title), body=body,\n                             status=status, post_type=post_type, author_id=editor[\"id\"])\n            print(\"Created sample posts\")\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n    # TODO (later tickets): listings.\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n# Copy to .env (which is gitignored) and change the values.\nCMS_SECRET_KEY=change-me-to-a-long-random-string\nCMS_SITE_TITLE=Knox County Historical Society\nCMS_DATABASE=cms.db\n# Demo accounts created by scripts/seed_demo.py\nCMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me-admin\nCMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD=change-me-editor\n# Set after you deploy, e.g. https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nCMS_BASE_PATH=\n\"\"\"Admin: one list of pages and posts together, filtered by status and type.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request\n\nfrom app import pages, posts\nfrom app.web import current_user, get_db, render\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/content\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n\n# The \"type\" filter: Page, or one of the Post Types.\nTYPES = {\"page\": \"Page\", **posts.POST_TYPES}\n\n\ndef _items(conn: sqlite3.Connection, status: str, type_: str) -> list[dict]:\n    items = [{\"kind\": \"page\", \"type\": \"page\", \"type_label\": \"Page\", **dict(p)}\n             for p in pages.list_pages(conn)]\n    items += [{\"kind\": \"post\", \"type\": p[\"post_type\"],\n               \"type_label\": posts.POST_TYPES[p[\"post_type\"]], **dict(p)}\n              for p in posts.list_posts(conn)]\n    items = [i for i in items\n             if (not status or i[\"status\"] == status) and (not type_ or i[\"type\"] == type_)]\n    return sorted(items, key=lambda i: (i[\"updated_at\"], i[\"id\"]), reverse=True)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef content_list(request: Request, status: str = \"\", type: str = \"\",\n                 user=Depends(current_user), conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    status = status if status in pages.STATUSES else \"\"\n    type_ = type if type in TYPES else \"\"\n    return render(request, \"admin/content_list.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Content\", \"items\": _items(conn, status, type_),\n                   \"status\": status, \"type\": type_, \"statuses\": pages.STATUSES,\n                   \"types\": TYPES}, user=user)\n101:    for route in _users_routes(create_app(database_path)):\n126:def _users_routes(app):\n127:    \"\"\"Every Users APIRoute, including those in included routers (FastAPI nests them).\"\"\"\n129:    for route in app.routes:\n130:        nested = getattr(route, \"original_router\", None)\n131:        for r in ([route] if isinstance(route, APIRoute) else nested.routes if nested else []):\n140:    routes = _users_routes(app)\n141:    assert len(routes) >= 5\n144:    for route in routes:\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"739156cb-20c4-4ceb-badb-29f186b59e89","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":7},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d52cdfcc-cd48-42df-bb05-f582bbb33181","session_id":"9d3ee875-b856-4c5f-8263-d5e667d38523","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"c452e23c-792e-4dd7-91fc-5a3784f2bd13","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Each test names the criterion it covers.\"\"\"\nimport re\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import db, pages, settings, users\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import _csrf_from\n\nEMAIL = \"secret.alum@alumni.test\"\n\n\ndef _token(c) -> str:\n    return _csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin/listings/new\"))\n\n\ndef _create(c, name=\"Pat Alum\", email=EMAIL, class_year=\"1999\", consented=\"on\",\n            consent_date=\"2026-09-01\", csrf=True):\n    data = {\"name\": name, \"email\": email, \"class_year\": class_year,\n            \"consent_date\": consent_date}\n    if consented:\n        data[\"consented\"] = consented\n    if csrf:\n        data[\"csrf_token\"] = _token(c)\n    return c.post(\"/admin/listings\", data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef _listing_id(response) -> int:\n    match = re.search(r\"/admin/listings/(\\d+)/edit\", response.headers[\"location\"])\n    assert match, response.headers\n    return int(match.group(1))\n\n\ndef _publish_network(database_path, tmp_path) -> Path:\n    conn = db.connect(database_path)\n    admin = users.get_by_email(conn, \"admin@example.test\")\n    pages.create(conn, title=\"Alumni Network\", slug=\"alumni-network\",\n                 body=\"Alumni who chose to be listed.\", status=\"published\",\n                 author_id=admin[\"id\"])\n    conn.close()\n    return render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database_path)\n\n\ndef _network_html(out: Path) -> str:\n    return (out / \"alumni-network.html\").read_text()\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"role\", [\"admin\", \"editor\"])\ndef test_editor_can_create_edit_delete_listing(client_as, role):\n    \"\"\"An editor can create, edit and delete a Listing; recorded-by is the user.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(role)\n    response = _create(c)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    listing_id = _listing_id(response)\n    edit = c.get(response.headers[\"location\"])\n    assert \"saved\" in edit.text.lower() and \"Pat Alum\" in edit.text\n    assert \"Recorded by\" in edit.text\n    assert (\"Matt Burdette\" if role == \"admin\" else \"Joe\") in edit.text\n\n    token = _csrf_from(edit)\n    saved = c.post(f\"/admin/listings/{listing_id}\", follow_redirects=False,\n                   data={\"name\": \"Pat Q. Alum\", \"email\": EMAIL, \"class_year\": \"\",\n                         \"consented\": \"on\", \"consent_date\": \"2026-09-02\",\n                         \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert saved.status_code == 303\n    assert \"Pat Q. Alum\" in c.get(\"/admin/listings\").text\n\n    gone = c.post(f\"/admin/listings/{listing_id}/delete\", data={\"csrf_token\": token},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n    assert gone.status_code == 303\n    assert \"Pat Q. Alum\" not in c.get(\"/admin/listings\").text\n\n\ndef test_listings_persist_across_restart(client_as, database_path):\n    \"\"\"Listings persist across a server restart.\"\"\"\n    _create(client_as(\"editor\"))\n    fresh = TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n    token = _csrf_from(fresh.get(\"/login\"))\n    fresh.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"editor@example.test\",\n                               \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert \"Pat Alum\" in fresh.get(\"/admin/listings\").text\n\n\ndef test_consented_without_date_cannot_be_saved(client_as, database_path, tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"A Listing without a consent date cannot be saved as consented.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = _create(c, name=\"No Date\", consent_date=\"\")\n    assert response.status_code == 422\n    assert \"consent date\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert \"No Date\" not in c.get(\"/admin/listings\").text\n\n\ndef test_listing_without_consent_is_not_published(client_as, database_path, tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"A Listing without consent is saved but not published.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert _create(c, name=\"Quiet Quincy\", consented=None, consent_date=\"\").status_code == 303\n    assert _create(c, name=\"Loud Larry\").status_code == 303\n    html = _network_html(_publish_network(database_path, tmp_path))\n    assert \"Loud Larry\" in html and \"Quiet Quincy\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_published_names_are_alphabetical_below_page_text(client_as, database_path, tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Names of consented Listings appear alphabetically, below the page's own text.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    for name in (\"Zed Zimmer\", \"adam Archer\", \"Mia Moore\"):\n        _create(c, name=name, email=f\"{name.split()[0].lower()}@alumni.test\")\n    html = _network_html(_publish_network(database_path, tmp_path))\n    positions = [html.index(n) for n in (\"adam Archer\", \"Mia Moore\", \"Zed Zimmer\")]\n    assert positions == sorted(positions)\n    assert html.index(\"Alumni who chose to be listed.\") < positions[0]\n\n\ndef test_delete_or_withdraw_removes_name_at_next_publish(client_as, database_path, tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Deleting a Listing or removing consent, then publishing, removes the name.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    keep = _listing_id(_create(c, name=\"Withdrawn Wes\"))\n    drop = _listing_id(_create(c, name=\"Deleted Dan\"))\n    assert \"Withdrawn Wes\" in _network_html(_publish_network(database_path, tmp_path))\n    token = _token(c)\n    c.post(f\"/admin/listings/{keep}\", follow_redirects=False,\n           data={\"name\": \"Withdrawn Wes\", \"email\": EMAIL, \"class_year\": \"\",\n                 \"consent_date\": \"\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    c.post(f\"/admin/listings/{drop}/delete\", data={\"csrf_token\": token})\n    html = _network_html(render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", database_path))\n    assert \"Withdrawn Wes\" not in html and \"Deleted Dan\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_contact_line_uses_site_setting(client_as, database_path, tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    \"\"\"The Alumni Contact Line appears and uses Joe's address from a site setting.\"\"\"\n    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"CONTACT_EMAIL\", \"joe.contact@kenyon.test\")\n    html = _network_html(_publish_network(database_path, tmp_path))\n    assert \"joe.contact@kenyon.test\" in html\n\n\ndef test_no_alumnus_email_anywhere_in_site(client_as, database_path, tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"No alumnus email appears anywhere in site/.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    _create(c, name=\"Pat Alum\", email=EMAIL)\n    _create(c, name=\"Quiet\", email=\"quiet@alumni.test\", consented=None, consent_date=\"\")\n    out = _publish_network(database_path, tmp_path)\n    for path in out.rglob(\"*\"):\n        if path.is_file():\n            text = path.read_text()\n            assert EMAIL not in text and \"quiet@alumni.test\" not in text, path\n\n\ndef test_listing_names_are_escaped(client_as, database_path, tmp_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    _create(c, name=\"<script>alert(1)</script>\")\n    assert \"<script>\" not in _network_html(_publish_network(database_path, tmp_path))\n\n\ndef test_listing_forms_need_csrf(client_as):\n    \"\"\"Every Listing form rejects a POST without a CSRF token.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert _create(c, csrf=False).status_code == 403\n    listing_id = _listing_id(_create(c))\n    assert c.post(f\"/admin/listings/{listing_id}\", data={\"name\": \"X\"}).status_code == 403\n    assert c.post(f\"/admin/listings/{listing_id}/delete\").status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_anonymous_redirected_to_login(client):\n    \"\"\"Anonymous visitors are redirected to login.\"\"\"\n    for method, path in ((\"get\", \"/admin/listings\"), (\"get\", \"/admin/listings/new\"),\n                         (\"post\", \"/admin/listings\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/listings/1\"),\n                         (\"post\", \"/admin/listings/1/delete\")):\n        r = getattr(client, method)(path, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert r.status_code == 303 and r.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\", path\nEOF\ncat app/pages.py | sed -n 1,30p","description":"Write T07 tests first {"parentUuid":"cb087840-a5b9-4284-a4eb-e48c093a335e","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfk222pKrmXk9LMZqxZJn","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GXQkZwctMkyYS7L1HWwDrE","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python3 - <<'EOF'\nimport re\np='app/db.py'; s=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace('''\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect''','''\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS listings (\n    id           INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    name         TEXT NOT NULL,\n    email        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    class_year   INTEGER,\n    consent_date TEXT,\n    recorded_by  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),\n    created_at   TEXT NOT NULL,\n    updated_at   TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect''',1)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\n\np='app/settings.py'; s=open(p).read()\ns+='''# Joe's public address for the Alumni Contact Line (a site setting, not a Listing field).\nCONTACT_EMAIL = os.environ.get(\"CMS_CONTACT_EMAIL\", \"editor@example.test\")\n'''\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\n\np='.env.example'; s=open(p).read()\ns+='# Second Editor address shown on the Alumni Network page\\nCMS_CONTACT_EMAIL=editor@example.test\\n'\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\n\np='app/main.py'; s=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace(\"from app.routes import auth, content, pages, posts, users as users_routes\",\"from app.routes import auth, content, listings, pages, posts, users as users_routes\")\ns=s.replace(\"    app.include_router(content.router)\\n\",\"    app.include_router(content.router)\\n    app.include_router(listings.router)\\n\")\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\n\np='templates/admin/layout.html'; s=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace('<a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New post</a>','<a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New post</a> · <a href=\"/admin/listings\">Listings</a>')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncat > app/listings.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Listings (alumni who opted in): data access. Routes call these; they never touch HTTP.\n\nADR-003: the export reads names only. `consented_names` is the only query the\npublisher uses, and it never selects the email column.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.pages import _now\n\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT listings.*, users.name AS recorder_name FROM listings \"\n           \"JOIN users ON users.id = listings.recorded_by\")\n\n\ndef list_listings(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY listings.name COLLATE NOCASE\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef get(conn: sqlite3.Connection, listing_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE listings.id = ?\", (listing_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef consented_names(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Names of consented Listings, alphabetical. Never reads the email.\"\"\"\n    rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT name FROM listings WHERE consent_date IS NOT NULL \"\n                        \"ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE, id\").fetchall()\n    return [r[\"name\"] for r in rows]\n\n\ndef create(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, name: str, email: str, class_year: int | None,\n           consent_date: str | None, recorded_by: int) -> int:\n    now = _now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO listings (name, email, class_year, consent_date, recorded_by,\"\n        \" created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (name, email, class_year, consent_date, recorded_by, now, now))\n    conn.commit()\n    assert cur.lastrowid is not None\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update(conn: sqlite3.Connection, listing_id: int, *, name: str, email: str,\n           class_year: int | None, consent_date: str | None) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE listings SET name = ?, email = ?, class_year = ?,\"\n                 \" consent_date = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (name, email, class_year, consent_date, _now(), listing_id))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef delete(conn: sqlite3.Connection, listing_id: int) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM listings WHERE id = ?\", (listing_id,))\n    conn.commit()\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/listings.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Admin: record and manage Alumni Network Listings.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import date\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import listings\nfrom app.web import current_user, flash, get_db, render, verify_csrf\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/listings\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\ncsrf_required = [Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\n\ndef _listing_or_404(conn: sqlite3.Connection, listing_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    listing = listings.get(conn, listing_id)\n    if listing is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such listing.\")\n    return listing\n\n\ndef _editor(request: Request, user, form: dict, listing: sqlite3.Row | None = None,\n            status_code: int = 200, error: str | None = None):\n    if error:\n        flash(request, error, \"error\")\n    return render(request, \"admin/listing_edit.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Edit listing\" if listing else \"New listing\",\n                   \"form\": form, \"listing\": listing}, status_code=status_code, user=user)\n\n\ndef _clean(name: str, email: str, class_year: str, consented: str,\n           consent_date: str) -> tuple[dict, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Normalise the submitted fields; return (fields, error).\"\"\"\n    fields = {\"name\": name.strip(), \"email\": email.strip(), \"class_year\": None,\n              \"consent_date\": consent_date.strip() if consented else None}\n    form = {**fields, \"class_year\": class_year.strip(), \"consented\": bool(consented),\n            \"consent_date\": consent_date.strip()}\n    if not fields[\"name\"]:\n        return form, \"A listing needs a name.\"\n    if \"@\" not in fields[\"email\"]:\n        return form, \"A listing needs an email address.\"\n    if form[\"class_year\"]:\n        if not (form[\"class_year\"].isdigit() and 1800 <= int(form[\"class_year\"]) <= 2100):\n            return form, \"The class year must be a four-digit year, or blank.\"\n        fields[\"class_year\"] = int(form[\"class_year\"])\n    if consented:\n        try:\n            date.fromisoformat(fields[\"consent_date\"] or \"\")\n        except ValueError:\n            return form, \"A consented listing needs a consent date (YYYY-MM-DD).\"\n    return {**form, **{k: fields[k] for k in (\"class_year\", \"consent_date\")},\n            \"_save\": fields}, None\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef list_all(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user),\n             conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    return render(request, \"admin/listing_list.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Listings\", \"items\": listings.list_listings(conn)}, user=user)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_listing(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return _editor(request, user, {\"name\": \"\", \"email\": \"\", \"class_year\": \"\",\n                                   \"consented\": False,\n                                   \"consent_date\": date.today().isoformat()})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=csrf_required)\ndef create_listing(request: Request, name: str = Form(\"\"), email: str = Form(\"\"),\n                   class_year: str = Form(\"\"), consented: str = Form(\"\"),\n                   consent_date: str = Form(\"\"), user=Depends(current_user),\n                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    form, error = _clean(name, email, class_year, consented, consent_date)\n    if error:\n        return _editor(request, user, form, status_code=422, error=error)\n    listing_id = listings.create(conn, recorded_by=user[\"id\"], **form[\"_save\"])\n    flash(request, f\"Listing “{form['name']}” saved.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/listings/{listing_id}/edit\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{listing_id}/edit\")\ndef edit_listing(listing_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(current_user),\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    listing = _listing_or_404(conn, listing_id)\n    form = {**dict(listing), \"class_year\": listing[\"class_year\"] or \"\",\n            \"consented\": listing[\"consent_date\"] is not None,\n            \"consent_date\": listing[\"consent_date\"] or date.today().isoformat()}\n    return _editor(request, user, form, listing)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{listing_id}\", dependencies=csrf_required)\ndef save_listing(listing_id: int, request: Request, name: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 email: str = Form(\"\"), class_year: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 consented: str = Form(\"\"), consent_date: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 user=Depends(current_user), conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    listing = _listing_or_404(conn, listing_id)\n    form, error = _clean(name, email, class_year, consented, consent_date)\n    if error:\n        return _editor(request, user, form, listing, status_code=422, error=error)\n    listings.update(conn, listing_id, **form[\"_save\"])\n    flash(request, f\"Listing “{form['name']}” saved.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/listings/{listing_id}/edit\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{listing_id}/delete\", dependencies=csrf_required)\ndef delete_listing(listing_id: int, request: Request,\n                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    listing = _listing_or_404(conn, listing_id)\n    listings.delete(conn, listing_id)\n    flash(request, f\"Listing “{listing['name']}” deleted.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/listings\", status_code=303)\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/listing_list.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Listings</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/listings/new\">New listing</a></p>\n  {% if items %}\n  <table>\n    <thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Class</th><th>Consent date</th><th>Recorded by</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n    {% for i in items %}\n      <tr>\n        <td><a href=\"/admin/listings/{{ i.id }}/edit\">{{ i.name }}</a></td>\n        <td>{{ i.class_year or '' }}</td>\n        <td>{{ i.consent_date or 'not consented (not published)' }}</td>\n        <td>{{ i.recorder_name }}</td>\n        <td>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/listings/{{ i.id }}/delete\" onsubmit=\"return confirm('Delete this listing?')\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">Delete</button>\n          </form>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n    {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>No listings yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/listing_edit.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ 'Edit listing' if listing else 'New listing' }}</h1>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ '/admin/listings/%d' % listing.id if listing else '/admin/listings' }}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <label for=\"name\">Name (the only thing published)</label>\n    <input id=\"name\" name=\"name\" value=\"{{ form.name }}\" required>\n    <label for=\"email\">Email (never published)</label>\n    <input id=\"email\" name=\"email\" type=\"email\" value=\"{{ form.email }}\" required>\n    <label for=\"class_year\">Class year (optional)</label>\n    <input id=\"class_year\" name=\"class_year\" value=\"{{ form.class_year }}\" inputmode=\"numeric\">\n    <label><input type=\"checkbox\" name=\"consented\" value=\"on\" {{ 'checked' if <dt>Recorded by</dt><dd>{{ listing.recorder_name }}</dd>\n    <dt>Created</dt><dd>{{ listing.created_at }}</dd>\n    <dt>Updated</dt><dd>{{ listing.updated_at }}</dd>\n  </dl>\n  {% endif %}\n{% Routes call these; they never touch HTTP.\n\nADR-003: the export reads names only. `consented_names` is the only query the\npublisher uses, and it never selects the email column.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.pages import _now\n\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT listings.*, users.name AS recorder_name FROM listings \"\n           \"JOIN users ON users.id = listings.recorded_by\")\n\n\ndef list_listings(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY listings.name COLLATE NOCASE\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef get(conn: sqlite3.Connection, listing_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE listings.id = ?\", (listing_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef consented_names(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Names of consented Listings, alphabetical. Never reads the email.\"\"\"\n    rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT name FROM listings WHERE consent_date IS NOT NULL \"\n                        \"ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE, id\").fetchall()\n    return [r[\"name\"] for r in rows]\n\n\ndef create(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, name: str, email: str, class_year: int | None,\n           consent_date: str | None, recorded_by: int) -> int:\n    now = _now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO listings (name, email, class_year, consent_date, recorded_by,\"\n        \" created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (name, email, class_year, consent_date, recorded_by, now, now))\n    conn.commit()\n    assert cur.lastrowid is not None\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update(conn: sqlite3.Connection, listing_id: int, *, name: str, email: str,\n           class_year: int | None, consent_date: str | None) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE listings SET name = ?, email = ?, class_year = ?,\"\n                 \" consent_date = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (name, email, class_year, consent_date, _now(), listing_id))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef delete(conn: sqlite3.Connection, listing_id: int) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM listings WHERE id = ?\", (listing_id,))\n    conn.commit()\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/listings.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Admin: record and manage Alumni Network Listings.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import date\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import listings\nfrom app.web import current_user, flash, get_db, render, verify_csrf\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/listings\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\ncsrf_required = [Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\n\ndef _listing_or_404(conn: sqlite3.Connection, listing_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    listing = listings.get(conn, listing_id)\n    if listing is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such listing.\")\n    return listing\n\n\ndef _editor(request: Request, user, form: dict, listing: sqlite3.Row | None = None,\n            status_code: int = 200, error: str | None = None):\n    if error:\n        flash(request, error, \"error\")\n    return render(request, \"admin/listing_edit.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Edit listing\" if listing else \"New listing\",\n                   \"form\": form, \"listing\": listing}, status_code=status_code, user=user)\n\n\ndef _clean(name: str, email: str, class_year: str, consented: str,\n           consent_date: str) -> tuple[dict, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Normalise the submitted fields; return (fields, error).\"\"\"\n    fields = {\"name\": name.strip(), \"email\": email.strip(), \"class_year\": None,\n              \"consent_date\": consent_date.strip() if consented else None}\n    form = {**fields, \"class_year\": class_year.strip(), \"consented\": bool(consented),\n            \"consent_date\": consent_date.strip()}\n    if not fields[\"name\"]:\n        return form, \"A listing needs a name.\"\n    if \"@\" not in fields[\"email\"]:\n        return form, \"A listing needs an email address.\"\n    if form[\"class_year\"]:\n        if not (form[\"class_year\"].isdigit() and 1800 <= int(form[\"class_year\"]) <= 2100):\n            return form, \"The class year must be a four-digit year, or blank.\"\n        fields[\"class_year\"] = int(form[\"class_year\"])\n    if consented:\n        try:\n            date.fromisoformat(fields[\"consent_date\"] or \"\")\n        except ValueError:\n            return form, \"A consented listing needs a consent date (YYYY-MM-DD).\"\n    return {**form, **{k: fields[k] for k in (\"class_year\", \"consent_date\")},\n            \"_save\": fields}, None\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef list_all(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user),\n             conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    return render(request, \"admin/listing_list.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Listings\", \"items\": listings.list_listings(conn)}, user=user)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_listing(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return _editor(request, user, {\"name\": \"\", \"email\": \"\", \"class_year\": \"\",\n                                   \"consented\": False,\n                                   \"consent_date\": date.today().isoformat()})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=csrf_required)\ndef create_listing(request: Request, name: str = Form(\"\"), email: str = Form(\"\"),\n                   class_year: str = Form(\"\"), consented: str = Form(\"\"),\n                   consent_date: str = Form(\"\"), user=Depends(current_user),\n                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    form, error = _clean(name, email, class_year, consented, consent_date)\n    if error:\n        return _editor(request, user, form, status_code=422, error=error)\n    listing_id = listings.create(conn, recorded_by=user[\"id\"], **form[\"_save\"])\n    flash(request, f\"Listing “{form['name']}” saved.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/listings/{listing_id}/edit\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{listing_id}/edit\")\ndef edit_listing(listing_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(current_user),\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    listing = _listing_or_404(conn, listing_id)\n    form = {**dict(listing), \"class_year\": listing[\"class_year\"] or \"\",\n            \"consented\": listing[\"consent_date\"] is not None,\n            \"consent_date\": listing[\"consent_date\"] or date.today().isoformat()}\n    return _editor(request, user, form, listing)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{listing_id}\", dependencies=csrf_required)\ndef save_listing(listing_id: int, request: Request, name: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 email: str = Form(\"\"), class_year: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 consented: str = Form(\"\"), consent_date: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 user=Depends(current_user), conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    listing = _listing_or_404(conn, listing_id)\n    form, error = _clean(name, email, class_year, consented, consent_date)\n    if error:\n        return _editor(request, user, form, listing, status_code=422, error=error)\n    listings.update(conn, listing_id, **form[\"_save\"])\n    flash(request, f\"Listing “{form['name']}” saved.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/listings/{listing_id}/edit\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{listing_id}/delete\", dependencies=csrf_required)\ndef delete_listing(listing_id: int, request: Request,\n                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    listing = _listing_or_404(conn, listing_id)\n    listings.delete(conn, listing_id)\n    flash(request, f\"Listing “{listing['name']}” deleted.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/listings\", status_code=303)\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/listing_list.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Listings</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/listings/new\">New listing</a></p>\n  {% if items %}\n  <table>\n    <thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Class</th><th>Consent date</th><th>Recorded by</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n    {% for i in items %}\n      <tr>\n        <td><a href=\"/admin/listings/{{ i.id }}/edit\">{{ i.name }}</a></td>\n        <td>{{ i.class_year or '' }}</td>\n        <td>{{ i.consent_date or 'not consented (not published)' }}</td>\n        <td>{{ i.recorder_name }}</td>\n        <td>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/listings/{{ i.id }}/delete\" onsubmit=\"return confirm('Delete this listing?')\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">Delete</button>\n          </form>\n        </td>\n      </tr>\n    {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>No listings yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/listing_edit.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n Computer use (desktop control) lets Claude take screenshots of the person's desktop and control it with clicks, typing and scrolling through the Claude desktop app; its tools are named mcp__computer-use__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__computer_* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before computer use is turned on for a conversation there may be no such tools, only an enable__mcp__remote-devices__computer tool, which turns it on. It covers turning it on, picking the right tool, the access flow, and the safety rules for tiered apps, links and financial actions. It is not for websites, which go through Claude in Chrome or the built-in browser and their own skills.\n- anthropic-skills:deep-research: Use this skill when the user's prompt requires (1) researching a topic across multiple sources, comparing options or alternatives, analyzing trends or history, understanding markets or industries, or reviewing literature or studies and (2) synthesizing that research into a comprehensive, narrative report. If you're planning to search the web or internal knowledge bases, consider using this skill. This skill coordinates research subagents, so use it only when you have a tool for spawning subagents (the Agent or Task tool); otherwise, research the question directly.\n</system-reminder>"}],"session_id":"9d3ee875-b856-4c5f-8263-d5e667d38523","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"c452e23c-792e-4dd7-91fc-5a3784f2bd13","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"eee5d786-ff75-4082-8243-357d303deace","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"29cf6a3e-5db1-4e01-b2dc-0ad2dbd3983c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/sphod/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/mattpocock-skills/1.2.3/skills/engineering/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nAnything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: since HEAD (uncommitted T07 changes); spec: issue #8 and #1"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"d2f0acf3-d9af-4d27-a740-cc99e6325dd5","timestamp":"2026-10-06T00:16:11.084Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01JcCjnvktFdpAThbmpvsxAQ","session_id":"9d3ee875-b856-4c5f-8263-d5e667d38523","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"c452e23c-792e-4dd7-91fc-5a3784f2bd13","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
