Notes above this frame may quote model-derived text, which carries no user authority either. The report follows:\n  T07 review (read-only; I did not run the tests).\n  \n  **Acceptance criteria**\n  1. CRUD with recorded-by: met. Routes and the create/edit/delete templates exist, and recorded_by = user[\"id\"]. The test covers both roles.\n  2. Persistence across a restart: met. SQLite table plus a fresh-app test.\n  3. \"A Listing without a consent date cannot be saved as consented, and is not published\": met, but see (c) for the control that tests consent.\n  4. Alphabetical names below the page's Markdown: met in the listing query and template. The test checks position against the body text.\n  5. Delete or withdraw, then publish, removes the name: met (test present).\n  6. \"The Alumni Contact Line ... uses Joe's address from a site setting\": met via CMS_CONTACT_EMAIL.\n  7. \"A test asserts that no alumnus email appears anywhere in `site/`\": met. It does not check the contact address, which is fine.\n  8. CSRF and anonymous redirect: met for all 5 listing routes, and the routes are covered by test_anonymous_redirected_to_login. The CSRF test only checks the unconsented-token case on one create route (plus update and delete). No gaps.\n  \n  **(a) Missing or partial**\n  - Spec story 13: \"counts of posts, pages, drafts and listings on a dashboard\". There is no Listings count, but this belongs to the dashboard ticket, not T07, so I'd leave it.\n  - Spec Testing Decisions: \"Enumerate the admin-only routes ... CSRF one per form\". The tests list the listing routes by hand. That is a minor gap against the spec, not a T07 criterion.\n  - \"Alumni Network ... Home, Alumni Events and Alumni Network recognised by fixed slugs\". The page's Markdown is rendered above the section only if the page is published. If the page is unpublished, listings are silently not shown. This matches the spec.\n  \n  **(b) Scope creep**\n  - Seed script: adds three sample Listings. Story 52 / \"Demo data ... sample Listings\" asks for this, so it is justified, but it is not in T07's criteria.\n  - Class-year validation (1800 to 2100) and an \"@\" check on the email are not in the ticket. They are small and reasonable.\n  - The `test_listing_names_are_escaped` test and a \"No alumni are listed yet\" empty state were not asked for. Both are harmless.\n  - The `consent_date` field defaults to today in the form. The spec does not ask for this.\n  \n  **(c) Implemented but possibly wrong**\n  - Model deviation: the form has a separate \"consented\" checkbox plus a date field. The spec says consent is a consent date (ADR-003: \"Only Listings with consent are exported\"). Ticket wording: \"A Listing without a consent date cannot be saved as consented\". If the checkbox is unticked but a date is entered, the date is silently discarded in `_clean` (`save[\"consent_date\"]` stays None). That is data loss without a warning.\n  - Contact Line text: story 34 says \"a single line ... telling me to email Joe\". The template says \"email {address} and we will introduce you\". It names an address, not Joe. Possibly ok, but check the wording against CONTEXT.md.\n  - The contact address is shown in a `mailto:` link on the public page. That is intended (Joe's address), but the default `editor@example.test` would publish a placeholder if the env var is unset.\n  - The consent date is validated only by `date.fromisoformat`. A future date is accepted.\n  - Minor: `from typing import Any` is placed before `from pathlib import Path` in publish.py (import order), and one line exceeds typical line length. This could trip a linter.\n  - The listing delete uses `confirm()` in `onsubmit` inline. That is fine, but check that your CSP does not forbid it.\n</agent-message>"}

Story 52 / \"Demo data ... sample Listings\" asks for this, so it is justified, but it is not in T07's criteria.\n  - Class-year validation (1800 to 2100) and an \"@\" check on the email are not in the ticket. They are small and reasonable.\n  - The `test_listing_names_are_escaped` test and a \"No alumni are listed yet\" empty state were not asked for. Both are harmless.\n  - The `consent_date` field defaults to today in the form. The spec does not ask for this.\n  \n  **(c) Implemented but possibly wrong**\n  - Model deviation: the form has a separate \"consented\" checkbox plus a date field. The spec says consent is a consent date (ADR-003: \"Only Listings with consent are exported\"). Ticket wording: \"A Listing without a consent date cannot be saved as consented\". If the checkbox is unticked but a date is entered, the date is silently discarded in `_clean` (`save[\"consent_date\"]` stays None). That is data loss without a warning.\n  - Contact Line text: story 34 says \"a single line ... telling me to email Joe\". The template says \"email {address} and we will introduce you\". It names an address, not Joe. Possibly ok, but check the wording against CONTEXT.md.\n  - The contact address is shown in a `mailto:` link on the public page. That is intended (Joe's address), but the default `editor@example.test` would publish a placeholder if the env var is unset.\n  - The consent date is validated only by `date.fromisoformat`. A future date is accepted.\n  - Minor: `from typing import Any` is placed before `from pathlib import Path` in publish.py (import order), and one line exceeds typical line length. This could trip a linter.\n  - The listing delete uses `confirm()` in `onsubmit` inline. That is fine, but check that your CSP does not forbid it.","handback":true},"promptSource":"system","turnOrigin":"peer","turnPosition":{"promptIndex":1,"turnIndex":2},"queueSkipAttachments":true,"userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"c452e23c-792e-4dd7-91fc-5a3784f2bd13","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Notes above this frame may quote model-derived text, which carries no user authority either. The report follows:\n  T07 review (read-only). Read the full diff, CONTEXT.md, ADR-003 and the new files. I did not run the tests.\n  \n  **(a) Documented-standard violations**\n  - None hard. Checked against CLAUDE.md and ADR-003:\n    - Both public-site changes are fine. `consented_names` selects `name` only (ADR-003), and `_sections.html` and `publish.py` pass only names plus `contact_email`. The `mailto:` has no leading `/`, so the relative-path rule holds.\n    - The three state-changing routes (`create_listing`, `save_listing`, `delete_listing`) use `csrf_required`, and both templates carry `csrf_token`.\n    - Escaping: Jinja autoescape covers names. There is no Markdown here, so sanitizing does not apply.\n    - `.env.example` holds only a placeholder address, and no secrets are added.\n  - Vocabulary in the new strings matches CONTEXT.md: Listing, Consent date, Alumni Contact Line, Second Editor.\n  - Process notes only: `docs/transcripts/*` are untracked and not part of this change. Remember the `T07:` / `Closes #N` commit format and the `/code-review` comment on the issue.\n  \n  **(b) Baseline smells (judgement calls)**\n  - **`app/listings.py`, `from app.pages import _now`.** This is Feature Envy / Mysterious Name. It imports a private helper across modules. `posts.py` already does the same, so it is repo precedent, but the import is now in three places. A shared `app/clock.py` would be cleaner.\n  - **`app/routes/listings.py`, `_clean(...)`.**\n    - Primitive Obsession: the returned `tuple[dict, dict, str | None]`, with the untyped `save: dict` and `form` dicts.\n    - Data Clump: `name, email, class_year, consented, consent_date` recur in `_clean`, `create_listing`, `save_listing` and `listings.create/update`.\n    - Duplicated Code: the identical signature of five `Form(...)` parameters in create and save, plus the same `flash(... saved.)` / redirect lines. A small `ListingForm` dataclass would fix all three.\n  - **`edit_listing` hunk:**\n    `form = {**dict(listing), \"class_year\": listing[\"class_year\"] or \"\", \"consented\": ..., \"consent_date\": ... or date.today().isoformat()}`\n    The same default (`date.today().isoformat()`) is also in `new_listing`. That is Duplicated Code and Primitive Obsession, since the dict shape is defined in two places.\n  - **`app/routes/listings.py`, `_editor(...)` and `_listing_or_404`.** These mirror `routes/pages.py` and `routes/posts.py` (`_page_or_404`, `_editor`, `csrf_required`). That is Duplicated Code / Shotgun Surgery risk across the three admin route modules. A generic `_or_404` helper is the likely fix.\n  - **`app/publish.py`:**\n    `sections_for: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {HOME_SLUG: ..., NETWORK_SLUG: {\"alumni\": alumni_names, \"contact_email\": settings.CONTACT_EMAIL}}`\n    - Repeated Switches: a per-slug dict plus an `{% if alumni is defined %}` / `updates is defined` ladder in `_sections.html`. Each new section needs edits in both places (Shotgun Surgery).\n    - Minor: `from typing import Any` is placed before `from pathlib import Path`, which is out of order, but ruff probably catches that.\n  - **`app/db.py`, `consent_date TEXT` nullable.** This is Primitive Obsession: consent is encoded as \"date is not NULL\", and `listing_list.html` shows the string \"not consented (not published)\". Judgement call only. The model is documented in CONTEXT.md as \"consent date\", so it is acceptable.\n  - **`templates/admin/layout.html` nav.** One more hard-coded link per content type is Shotgun Surgery, but it is minor.\n  - No Middle Man, Speculative Generality or Refused Bequest found.\n</agent-message>"}

{"parentUuid":"4a4d83da-9357-471d-84b1-09925d06755c","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfk2FLChf32wgTV9zMB7z","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"T07 is committed as `6353b20` on `main`, with a message ending `Closes #8`. The full suite passes (127 tests). mypy shows only the 3 errors in `tests/test_usage_report.py` that were already there.\n\n- **Listings:** editors can create, edit and delete Listings under `/admin/listings`, with a \"Listings\" link in the admin nav.\n  - Recorded-by is set to the logged-in user.\n  - Every POST needs a CSRF token, and anonymous visitors are redirected to login.\n  - The `listings` table is created at startup, so Listings survive a restart.\n- **Consent rule:** a Listing ticked as consented must have a valid consent date, otherwise the save is refused. Unticking consent clears the date, and the name stops being published.\n- **Publish:** the Alumni Network page shows consented names alphabetically, below the page's own Markdown. Only the name column is ever read, so no alumnus email reaches `site/`.\n- **Contact line:** the page tells visitors to \"email Joe at\" an address taken from the new `CMS_CONTACT_EMAIL` setting. It defaults to the placeholder `editor@example.test`, and `.env.example` documents it, so set the real address before deploying.\n- **Seed script:** it now adds three sample Listings, one without consent.\n- **Tests:** `tests/test_t07_listings.py` has 12 tests that map to the ticket's acceptance criteria.\n\nThe `/code-review` found no hard standards violations and no missing acceptance criteria. I fixed two things from it: the contact line now names Joe, and I corrected an import order. I posted the findings and how each was resolved on [issue #8](https://github.com/samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/8#issuecomment-6006266894), as CLAUDE.md requires. I deferred three items to T08: the dashboard Listings count, the route enumeration for CSRF tests, and a shared clock helper (the private `_now` import across modules).\n\nNo `Backend:` trailer was added because `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` is unset. I couldn't run `/status` myself.\n\n`notes/usage-ledger.csv` and the transcripts under `docs/transcripts/` were already uncommitted before this session. I left them out of the commit."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":482,"cache_read_input_tokens":88281,"output_tokens":760,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":482,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":760,"cache_read_input_tokens":88281,"cache_creation_input_tokens":482,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":482},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"fe4d8c73-05e0-4fd6-ab46-acd042592952","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011Cfk2FKq8Jof8Wtd1yeAmP","type":"assistant","uuid":"18ec58cc-eb75-4602-bf26-3ff6db52d24a","timestamp":"2026-10-06T00:17:24.695Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"9d3ee875-b856-4c5f-8263-d5e667d38523","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"c452e23c-792e-4dd7-91fc-5a3784f2bd13","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
